        { id: 'imovel', label: 'Consórcio de Imóveis', icon: '🏠', ranges: ['R$ 150 mil a R$ 300 mil', 'R$ 300 mil a R$ 600 mil', 'R$ 600 mil a R$ 1,2 milhão', 'Acima de R$ 1,2 milhão'] },
        { id: 'veiculo', label: 'Consórcio de Automóveis', icon: '🚗', ranges: ['R$ 40 mil a R$ 80 mil', 'R$ 80 mil a R$ 150 mil', 'R$ 150 mil a R$ 250 mil', 'Acima de R$ 250 mil'] },
        { id: 'pesados', label: 'Caminhões & Pesados', icon: '🚛', ranges: ['R$ 180 mil a R$ 350 mil', 'R$ 350 mil a R$ 600 mil', 'R$ 600 mil a R$ 1 milhão', 'Acima de R$ 1 milhão'] },
        { id: 'agro', label: 'Agronegócio & Maquinário', icon: '🚜', ranges: ['R$ 200 mil a R$ 500 mil', 'R$ 500 mil a R$ 1 milhão', 'R$ 1 milhão a R$ 3 milhões', 'Acima de R$ 3 milhões'] },
        { id: 'alavancagem', label: 'Alavancagem / Investimento', icon: '📈', ranges: ['R$ 250 mil a R$ 500 mil', 'R$ 500 mil a R$ 1 milhão', 'Acima de R$ 1 milhão'] }
      ];

      var strategies = [
        '🎯 Lance Fixo 40% + Lance Embutido 30% (Campanha Porto Week)',
        '💡 Lance Embutido (Usar até 30% da própria carta)',
        '📄 Usar saldo do FGTS como lance',
        '🚀 Ofertar lance com recurso próprio',
        '⏳ Aguardar sorteio com menor custo mensal'
      ];

      var urgencies = [
        '🔥 Urgente (próximos 30 a 60 dias)',
        '📅 Planejando para os próximos 6 meses',
        '🎯 Estratégia para o próximo ano'
      ];

      var consultantImg = 'https://zonneconsorcio.com.br/assets/consultor-lucas.jpg';

      window.toggleLeadBotModal = function() {
        var modal = document.getElementById('leadbot-modal');
        var backdrop = document.getElementById('leadbot-backdrop');
        var trigger = document.getElementById('leadbot-trigger-container');
        var mobileDock = document.getElementById('leadbot-mobile-dock');
        var messagesContainer = document.getElementById('leadbot-messages-container');
        if (!modal) return;
        var isCurrentlyHidden = modal.classList.contains('hidden') || modal.style.display === 'none';
        if (isCurrentlyHidden) {
          modal.classList.remove('hidden');
          modal.style.display = 'flex';
          if (backdrop) {
            backdrop.classList.remove('hidden');
          }
          if (trigger) {
            trigger.style.display = 'none';
            trigger.classList.remove('sm:flex');
            trigger.classList.add('hidden');
          }
          if (mobileDock) {
            mobileDock.style.display = 'none';
            mobileDock.classList.remove('block');
            mobileDock.classList.add('hidden');
          }
          if (currentStep === 1) renderStep1();
          if (messagesContainer && currentStep === 1) {
            messagesContainer.scrollTop = 0;
          }
        } else {
          modal.classList.add('hidden');
          modal.style.display = 'none';
          if (backdrop) {
            backdrop.classList.add('hidden');
          }
          if (trigger) {
            trigger.style.display = '';
            trigger.classList.add('hidden', 'sm:flex');
          }
          if (mobileDock) {
            mobileDock.style.display = '';
            mobileDock.classList.remove('hidden');
            mobileDock.classList.add('block', 'sm:hidden');
          }
        }
      };

      window.resetLeadBot = function() {
        currentStep = 1;
        botData = {};
        var container = document.getElementById('leadbot-messages-container');
        if (container) {
          container.innerHTML = 
            '<div class="flex items-start gap-2 max-w-[92%]">' +
              '<img src="' + consultantImg + '" alt="Lucas" class="w-7 h-7 rounded-full object-cover shrink-0 mt-0.5 border border-orange-200" onerror="this.src=\'/assets/consultor-lucas.jpg\'" />' +
              '<div class="max-w-[88%] p-3.5 rounded-2xl shadow-xs leading-relaxed text-[12px] sm:text-[13px] bg-white text-gray-800 border border-gray-200/90 rounded-tl-none">' +
                '👋 Olá! Sou o <strong>Lucas</strong>, consultor especialista da <strong>Zonne Consórcios</strong>. Analiso diariamente grupos nas 18 administradoras oficiais autorizadas pelo Banco Central para encontrar as melhores taxas e contemplações sem juros.' +
              '</div>' +
            '</div>' +
            '<div class="flex items-start gap-2 max-w-[92%]">' +
              '<img src="' + consultantImg + '" alt="Lucas" class="w-7 h-7 rounded-full object-cover shrink-0 mt-0.5 border border-orange-200" onerror="this.src=\'/assets/consultor-lucas.jpg\'" />' +
              '<div class="max-w-[88%] p-3.5 rounded-2xl shadow-xs leading-relaxed text-[12px] sm:text-[13px] bg-white text-gray-800 border border-gray-200/90 rounded-tl-none">' +
                'Qual tipo de conquista você deseja planejar hoje?' +
              '</div>' +
            '</div>' +
            '<div id="leadbot-step-content" class="pt-2"></div>';
        }
        updateStepBadge(1);
        renderStep1();
      };

      function updateStepBadge(step) {
        var badge = document.getElementById('leadbot-step-badge');
        if (badge) badge.innerText = typeof step === 'number' ? 'Passo ' + step + ' de 6' : step;
      }

      function appendUserMessage(text) {
        var container = document.getElementById('leadbot-messages-container');
        var stepContent = document.getElementById('leadbot-step-content');
        var msgDiv = document.createElement('div');
        msgDiv.className = 'flex flex-col items-end';
        msgDiv.innerHTML = '<div class="max-w-[85%] p-3.5 rounded-2xl rounded-tr-none shadow-xs leading-relaxed text-[12px] sm:text-[13px] bg-[#ff6a00] text-white font-medium">' + text + '</div>';
        container.insertBefore(msgDiv, stepContent);
        container.scrollTop = container.scrollHeight;
      }

      function appendBotMessage(text, callback) {
        var container = document.getElementById('leadbot-messages-container');
        var stepContent = document.getElementById('leadbot-step-content');
        stepContent.innerHTML = '<div class="flex items-center gap-2"><img src="' + consultantImg + '" alt="Lucas" class="w-6 h-6 rounded-full object-cover shrink-0 border border-orange-200" onerror="this.src=\'/assets/consultor-lucas.jpg\'" /><div class="flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-white border border-gray-200 px-3 py-2 rounded-2xl w-20 shadow-xs"><span class="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-gray-400 animate-bounce"></span><span class="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-gray-400 animate-bounce"></span><span class="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-gray-400 animate-bounce"></span></div></div>';
        container.scrollTop = container.scrollHeight;

        setTimeout(function() {
          var msgDiv = document.createElement('div');
          msgDiv.className = 'flex items-start gap-2 max-w-[92%]';
          msgDiv.innerHTML = '<img src="' + consultantImg + '" alt="Lucas" class="w-7 h-7 rounded-full object-cover shrink-0 mt-0.5 border border-orange-200" onerror="this.src=\'/assets/consultor-lucas.jpg\'" /><div class="max-w-[88%] p-3.5 rounded-2xl shadow-xs leading-relaxed text-[12px] sm:text-[13px] bg-white text-gray-800 border border-gray-200/90 rounded-tl-none">' + text + '</div>';
          container.insertBefore(msgDiv, stepContent);
          stepContent.innerHTML = '';
          container.scrollTop = container.scrollHeight;
          if (callback) callback();
        }, 500);
      }

      function renderStep1() {
        updateStepBadge(1);
        var stepContent = document.getElementById('leadbot-step-content');
        if (!stepContent) return;
        var html = '<span class="text-[10px] uppercase font-extrabold text-gray-500 tracking-wider block mb-1.5">Selecione o seu objetivo:</span><div class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-1.5">';
        categories.forEach(function(cat) {
          html += '<button onclick="window.selectCategory(\'' + cat.id + '\')" class="flex items-center justify-between px-3 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-white border border-gray-200 hover:border-[#ff6a00] hover:bg-orange-50 text-gray-800 hover:text-[#ff6a00] transition font-semibold text-[11px] text-left shadow-xs cursor-pointer"><span>' + cat.icon + ' ' + cat.label + '</span><span>→</span></button>';
        });
        html += '</div>';
        stepContent.innerHTML = html;
      }

      window.selectCategory = function(catId) {
        var found = categories.find(function(c) { return c.id === catId; });
        if (!found) return;
        botData.category = found.label;
        botData.categoryObj = found;
        appendUserMessage(found.icon + ' ' + found.label);
        var replyText = catId === 'esquenta_porto' 
          ? 'Excelente escolha! Na Campanha Esquenta Porto Week (Porto Bank), você tem 50% de redução na parcela até a contemplação, lance fixo de 40% e lance embutido de até 30% do crédito. Selecione o crédito desejado na tabela oficial:' 
          : 'Excelente escolha! Para ' + found.label.toLowerCase() + ', temos condições exclusivas. Qual faixa de crédito pretendida melhor atende ao seu objetivo?';
        appendBotMessage(replyText, function() {
          renderStep2();
        });
      };

      function renderStep2() {
        updateStepBadge(2);
        var stepContent = document.getElementById('leadbot-step-content');
        var cat = botData.categoryObj || categories[0];
        var html = '<span class="text-[10px] uppercase font-extrabold text-gray-500 tracking-wider block mb-1.5">Selecione a faixa de crédito:</span><div class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-1.5">';
        cat.ranges.forEach(function(r) {
          html += '<button onclick="window.selectRange(\'' + r + '\')" class="flex items-center justify-between px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-white border border-gray-200 hover:border-[#ff6a00] hover:bg-orange-50 text-gray-800 hover:text-[#ff6a00] transition font-semibold text-[11px] shadow-xs cursor-pointer"><span>' + r + '</span><span class="text-[10px] text-emerald-600 font-bold bg-emerald-50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md">0% Juros</span></button>';
        });
        html += '</div>';
        stepContent.innerHTML = html;
      }

      window.selectRange = function(range) {
        botData.creditRange = range;
        appendUserMessage('💰 Faixa de Crédito: ' + range);
        appendBotMessage('Perfeito! Trabalhamos com inteligência de grupos para não depender apenas de sorteio. Qual seria sua estratégia inicial para contemplação?', function() {
          renderStep3();
        });
      };

      function renderStep3() {
        updateStepBadge(3);
        var stepContent = document.getElementById('leadbot-step-content');
        var isImovel = botData.categoryObj && botData.categoryObj.id === 'imovel';
        var availableStrategies = strategies.filter(function(st) {
          if (st.indexOf('FGTS') !== -1) {
            return isImovel;
          }
          return true;
        });
        var html = '<span class="text-[10px] uppercase font-extrabold text-gray-500 tracking-wider block mb-1.5">Qual sua estratégia para contemplar?</span><div class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-1.5">';
        availableStrategies.forEach(function(st) {
          html += '<button onclick="window.selectStrategy(\'' + st + '\')" class="px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-white border border-gray-200 hover:border-[#ff6a00] hover:bg-orange-50 text-gray-800 hover:text-[#ff6a00] transition font-semibold text-[11px] text-left shadow-xs cursor-pointer">' + st + '</button>';
        });
        html += '</div>';
        stepContent.innerHTML = html;
      }

      window.selectStrategy = function(strat) {
        botData.strategy = strat;
        appendUserMessage(strat);
        appendBotMessage('Ótimo! Essa informação é fundamental para filtrar grupos com histórico de lance favorável. Em quanto tempo você planeja ser contemplado?', function() {
          renderStep4();
        });
      };

      function renderStep4() {
        updateStepBadge(4);
        var stepContent = document.getElementById('leadbot-step-content');
        var html = '<span class="text-[10px] uppercase font-extrabold text-gray-500 tracking-wider block mb-1.5">Quando deseja usufruir do bem?</span><div class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-1.5">';
        urgencies.forEach(function(u) {
          html += '<button onclick="window.selectUrgency(\'' + u + '\')" class="px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-white border border-gray-200 hover:border-[#ff6a00] hover:bg-orange-50 text-gray-800 hover:text-[#ff6a00] transition font-semibold text-[11px] text-left shadow-xs cursor-pointer">' + u + '</button>';
        });
        html += '</div>';
        stepContent.innerHTML = html;
      }

      window.selectUrgency = function(urg) {
        botData.urgency = urg;
        appendUserMessage(urg);
        appendBotMessage('Triagem concluída com sucesso! Localizei os 3 grupos com menor taxa de administração e histórico recente de lances favorável ao seu objetivo. Para quem devo direcionar este estudo exclusivo? Como posso te chamar?', function() {
          renderStep5();
        });
      };

      // STEP 5: Name Capture
      function renderStep5() {
        updateStepBadge(5);
        var stepContent = document.getElementById('leadbot-step-content');
        var html = 
          '<form onsubmit="window.submitLeadName(event)" class="space-y-2 bg-white p-3.5 rounded-2xl border border-orange-200 shadow-sm">' +
            '<div class="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[#ff6a00] font-bold text-xs">' +
              '👤 <span>Como posso te chamar?</span>' +
            '</div>' +
            '<div class="flex items-center gap-2 border border-gray-300 focus-within:border-[#ff6a00] rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 bg-white">' +
              '<input type="text" id="leadbot-name-input" required placeholder="Digite seu nome..." class="w-full text-base sm:text-xs outline-none bg-transparent text-gray-800" />' +
              '<button type="submit" class="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-[#ff6a00] hover:bg-[#e55f00] text-white font-bold text-xs cursor-pointer shrink-0">Continuar ➔</button>' +
            '</div>' +
          '</form>';
        stepContent.innerHTML = html;
        setTimeout(function() {
          var input = document.getElementById('leadbot-name-input');
          if (input) input.focus();
        }, 100);
      }

      window.submitLeadName = function(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        var nameInput = document.getElementById('leadbot-name-input');
        var name = nameInput ? nameInput.value.trim() : '';
        if (!name || name.length < 2) {
          alert('Por favor, informe seu nome para continuarmos.');
          return;
        }
        botData.name = name;
        appendUserMessage(name);

        var firstName = name.split(' ')[0];
        appendBotMessage('Prazer em falar com você, ' + firstName + '! Para finalizar e eu te enviar a análise dos grupos com sigilo absoluto, qual é o seu WhatsApp com DDD?', function() {
          renderStep6();
        });
      };

      // Phone Mask Helper
      window.maskPhoneInput = function(el) {
        var v = el.value.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 11);
        if (v.length <= 2) {
          el.value = v.length > 0 ? '(' + v : '';
        } else if (v.length <= 6) {
          el.value = '(' + v.slice(0, 2) + ') ' + v.slice(2);
        } else if (v.length <= 10) {
          el.value = '(' + v.slice(0, 2) + ') ' + v.slice(2, 6) + '-' + v.slice(6);
        } else {
          el.value = '(' + v.slice(0, 2) + ') ' + v.slice(2, 7) + '-' + v.slice(7, 11);
        }
      };

      // STEP 6: WhatsApp Capture
      function renderStep6() {
        updateStepBadge(6);
        var stepContent = document.getElementById('leadbot-step-content');
        var html = 
          '<form onsubmit="window.submitLeadPhone(event)" class="space-y-2 bg-white p-3.5 rounded-2xl border border-orange-200 shadow-sm">' +
            '<div class="flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-bold text-emerald-800">' +
              '<span>📱 Qual é o seu WhatsApp com DDD?</span>' +
              '<span class="text-[10px] text-gray-400 font-normal">Ex: 21 98888-8888</span>' +
            '</div>' +
            '<div class="flex items-center gap-2 border border-gray-300 focus-within:border-emerald-500 rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 bg-white">' +
              '<input type="tel" id="leadbot-phone-input" required placeholder="(21) 98888-8888" oninput="window.maskPhoneInput(this)" class="w-full text-base sm:text-xs outline-none bg-transparent text-gray-800" />' +
              '<button type="submit" id="leadbot-phone-btn" class="px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-gradient-to-r from-[#ff6a00] to-[#ea580c] text-white font-bold text-xs cursor-pointer shrink-0">Receber ➔</button>' +
            '</div>' +
            '<p class="text-[10px] text-gray-500 leading-tight">🔒 Enviaremos a simulação e os grupos direto no seu WhatsApp sem compromisso.</p>' +
          '</form>';
        stepContent.innerHTML = html;
        setTimeout(function() {
          var input = document.getElementById('leadbot-phone-input');
          if (input) input.focus();
        }, 100);
      }

      window.submitLeadPhone = function(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        var phoneInput = document.getElementById('leadbot-phone-input');
        var submitBtn = document.getElementById('leadbot-phone-btn');
        var phone = phoneInput ? phoneInput.value.trim() : '';

        var rawDigits = phone.replace(/\D/g, '');
        if (rawDigits.length < 10) {
          alert('Por favor, informe seu WhatsApp com DDD válido (ex: 11 98888-8888).');
          return;
        }

        botData.phone = phone;
        appendUserMessage(phone);

        if (submitBtn) {
          submitBtn.disabled = true;
          submitBtn.innerHTML = '⏳ Enviando...';
        }

        var leadPayload = {
          name: botData.name || 'Interessado(a)',
          phone: phone,
          email: 'Não informado',
          category: botData.category || 'Consórcio Geral',
          creditRange: botData.creditRange || 'A definir',
          strategy: botData.strategy || 'Lance Embutido / Flexível',
          urgency: botData.urgency || 'Próximos meses',
          originUrl: window.location.href
        };

        // Salvar em backup local
        try {
          var history = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('zonne_leads_history') || '[]');
          history.push(leadPayload);
          localStorage.setItem('zonne_leads_history', JSON.stringify(history));
        } catch (err) {}

        // Disparo assíncrono para o script PHP da HostGator (send-lead.php para contato@zonneconsorcio.com.br e delon.leo@hotmail.com)
        fetch('/send-lead.php', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Accept': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify(leadPayload)
        }).catch(function(err) {
          console.warn('Disparo de lead:', err);
        }).finally(function() {
          updateStepBadge('Finalizado ✨');
          var stepContent = document.getElementById('leadbot-step-content');
          if (stepContent) {
            stepContent.innerHTML = 
              '<div class="bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 p-4 rounded-2xl text-center space-y-2.5 animate-fadeIn">' +
                '<div class="w-10 h-10 bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-600 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto text-lg">✅</div>' +
                '<div>' +
                  '<h5 class="font-extrabold text-xs text-emerald-950">Solicitação Recebida com Sucesso!</h5>' +
                  '<p class="text-[11px] text-emerald-800 leading-snug mt-1">Seus dados foram enviados diretamente para a nossa equipe oficial:</p>' +
                  '<div class="inline-block bg-white/90 border border-emerald-300 rounded-lg px-2.5 py-1 text-[10px] font-mono font-bold text-emerald-900 mt-1.5">' +
                    'contato@zonneconsorcio.com.br' +
                  '</div>' +
                '</div>' +
                '<div class="bg-white rounded-xl p-2.5 border border-emerald-200/80 text-left space-y-1 text-[10px]">' +
                  '<div class="flex justify-between border-b border-gray-100 pb-0.5"><span class="text-gray-500">Nome:</span><span class="font-bold text-gray-800">' + (botData.name || 'Cliente') + '</span></div>' +
                  '<div class="flex justify-between border-b border-gray-100 pb-0.5"><span class="text-gray-500">WhatsApp:</span><span class="font-bold text-gray-800">' + phone + '</span></div>' +
                  '<div class="flex justify-between border-b border-gray-100 pb-0.5"><span class="text-gray-500">Objetivo:</span><span class="font-bold text-[#ff6a00]">' + (botData.category || 'Consórcio') + '</span></div>' +
                  '<div class="flex justify-between"><span class="text-gray-500">Crédito:</span><span class="font-bold text-emerald-700">' + (botData.creditRange || 'A definir') + '</span></div>' +
                '</div>' +
                '<p class="text-[10px] text-gray-600 leading-snug">Nossa equipe já está analisando as 18 administradoras e entrará em contato pelo seu WhatsApp com os melhores grupos.</p>' +
                '<button onclick="resetLeadBot()" class="text-[11px] font-bold text-[#ff6a00] hover:underline pt-1 cursor-pointer block mx-auto">Fazer nova consulta</button>' +
              '</div>';
          }
        });
      };

    })();
  </script>

  <!-- Pure JS Simulator & Accordion Script -->
  <script>
    const adminTaxRate = 14;
    const reserveFundRate = 2;
    const financingInterestRate = 17.5;
    const categoryName = "Consórcio de Automóveis & Veículos Leves";
    const phoneRaw = "5521973801284";

    const creditRange = document.getElementById('creditRange');
    const monthsRange = document.getElementById('monthsRange');
    const creditDisplay = document.getElementById('creditDisplay');
    const monthsDisplay = document.getElementById('monthsDisplay');
    const consorcioInstallmentDisplay = document.getElementById('consorcioInstallmentDisplay');
    const financingInstallmentDisplay = document.getElementById('financingInstallmentDisplay');
    const savingsDisplay = document.getElementById('savingsDisplay');
    const simWhatsAppBtn = document.getElementById('simWhatsAppBtn');
    const heroSimWhatsappBtn = document.getElementById('heroSimWhatsappBtn');

    const tableCreditDisplay = document.getElementById('tableCreditDisplay');
    const tableMonthsDisplay = document.getElementById('tableMonthsDisplay');
    const tableConsorcioInstallment = document.getElementById('tableConsorcioInstallment');
    const tableFinancingInstallment = document.getElementById('tableFinancingInstallment');
    const tableConsorcioTotal = document.getElementById('tableConsorcioTotal');
    const tableFinancingTotal = document.getElementById('tableFinancingTotal');
    const tableSavingsHighlight = document.getElementById('tableSavingsHighlight');

    function formatBrl(val) {
      return new Intl.NumberFormat('pt-BR', { style: 'currency', currency: 'BRL', maximumFractionDigits: 0 }).format(val);
    }

    function calculateConsorcio(credit, months) {
      const adminFeeTotal = credit * (adminTaxRate / 100);
      const reserveFundTotal = credit * (reserveFundRate / 100);
      const totalCost = credit + adminFeeTotal + reserveFundTotal;
      const monthlyInstallment = totalCost / months;
      return { monthlyInstallment, adminFeeTotal, totalCost };
    }

    function calculateFinancing(credit, months) {
      const annualRate = financingInterestRate / 100;
      const monthlyRate = Math.pow(1 + annualRate, 1 / 12) - 1;
      const factor = Math.pow(1 + monthlyRate, months);
      const monthlyPayment = (credit * monthlyRate * factor) / (factor - 1);
      const totalFinancing = monthlyPayment * months;
      return { monthlyPayment, totalFinancing };
    }

    function updateSimulator() {
      const credit = parseFloat(creditRange.value);
      const months = parseInt(monthsRange.value, 10);
      const years = Math.floor(months / 12);

      creditDisplay.textContent = formatBrl(credit);
      monthsDisplay.textContent = months + ' meses' + (years > 0 ? ' (' + years + ' anos)' : '');

      const cons = calculateConsorcio(credit, months);
      const fin = calculateFinancing(credit, months);
      const savings = fin.totalFinancing - cons.totalCost;

      consorcioInstallmentDisplay.innerHTML = formatBrl(cons.monthlyInstallment) + '<span class="text-xs text-gray-400 font-normal">/mês</span>';
      financingInstallmentDisplay.textContent = formatBrl(fin.monthlyPayment) + '/mês';
      savingsDisplay.textContent = formatBrl(savings);

      if (tableCreditDisplay) tableCreditDisplay.textContent = formatBrl(credit);
      if (tableMonthsDisplay) tableMonthsDisplay.textContent = months + ' meses';
      if (tableConsorcioInstallment) tableConsorcioInstallment.textContent = formatBrl(cons.monthlyInstallment) + '/mês';
      if (tableFinancingInstallment) tableFinancingInstallment.textContent = formatBrl(fin.monthlyPayment) + '/mês';
      if (tableConsorcioTotal) tableConsorcioTotal.textContent = formatBrl(cons.totalCost);
      if (tableFinancingTotal) tableFinancingTotal.textContent = formatBrl(fin.totalFinancing);
      if (tableSavingsHighlight) tableSavingsHighlight.textContent = 'Você economiza ' + formatBrl(savings) + ' contratando com a Zonne';

      const message = encodeURIComponent('Olá! Simulei uma carta de ' + formatBrl(credit) + ' em ' + months + ' meses no ' + categoryName + ' (parcela de ' + formatBrl(cons.monthlyInstallment) + '). Gostaria de atendimento com os grupos disponíveis.');
      if (simWhatsAppBtn) simWhatsAppBtn.href = 'https://wa.me/' + phoneRaw + '?text=' + message;
      if (heroSimWhatsappBtn) heroSimWhatsappBtn.href = 'https://wa.me/' + phoneRaw + '?text=' + message;
    }

    if (creditRange && monthsRange) {
      creditRange.addEventListener('input', updateSimulator);
      monthsRange.addEventListener('input', updateSimulator);
    }

    function toggleCategoryMobileMenu() {
      const panel = document.getElementById('category-mobile-menu-panel');
      if (panel) {
        panel.classList.toggle('hidden');
      }
    }

    function toggleFaq(index) {
      const answer = document.getElementById('faqAnswer-' + index);
      const icon = document.getElementById('faqIcon-' + index);
      if (!answer || !icon) return;

      const isHidden = answer.classList.contains('hidden');
      if (isHidden) {
        answer.classList.remove('hidden');
        answer.classList.add('block');
        icon.textContent = '−';
      } else {
        answer.classList.remove('block');
        answer.classList.add('hidden');
        icon.textContent = '+';
      }
    }
  </script>

</body></html>
